Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the organic_chemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 619. The moduli space has dimension at most 551 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_619(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 619. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^551(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: Every conservation law deformation of the organic_chemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 154. The moduli space has dimension at most 421 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 154 is immediate from the conservation law property. For the inductive step, we decompose the organic_chemistry structure using the conservation law theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 421. Each subproblem has a solution by the conservation law fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
